The analyst believes a dividend is unlikely in the next few years as Netflix needs to rebuild cash and reduce buyback pace.
<<<INSIGHTS>>>

The stock price has declined 45% from its 52-week high, and the analyst sees no dividend likelihood in the near term, presenting a mixed outlook.
META | positive | Meta is mentioned as a positive comparison point, having declared its first dividend in February 2024 from a much stronger financial position with $65.4 billion in cash versus $18.4 billion in debt, and $43 billion in free cash flow. headline: Fed Gov. Michelle Bowman: Risk of "Large Correction" in Stocks if AI Doesn't Pan Out
<<<SUMMARY>>>
Federal Reserve Governor Michelle Bowman warned of a potential "large correction" in stock prices if the hundreds of billions of dollars invested in AI capital expenditures fail to deliver adequate returns. She highlighted risks including over $800 billion in AI capex financed through corporate bonds and potential "circular financing" among AI companies. A tech stock decline could trigger a broader economic slowdown through reduced consumer spending.
